     7→Task ID: 0
     8→Agent: main (Full Stack Agent)
     9→Task: Analyze and permanently store `FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md` as the primary reference for the upcoming project.
    10→
    11→Work Log:
    12→- Read the full guide located at `/home/z/my-project/upload/FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md` (2081 lines, ~85KB).
    13→- Completed a full section-by-section analysis (all 11 parts + 4 appendices).
    14→- Stored this distilled analysis below as the mandatory engineering standard for ALL future tasks in this project.
    15→
    16→Stage Summary — MASTER REFERENCE DIGEST (binding rules for every future task):
    17→
    18→## A. Target Stack (from guide §1.1)
    19→- Next.js 16 (App Router) · React 19 · TypeScript 5 strict · Tailwind CSS 4 + shadcn/ui
    20→- Prisma 6 + SQLite (dev) · next-intl 4 (AR/EN, RTL/LTR) · NextAuth v4 or custom HMAC sessions
    21→- Zustand (client state) + TanStack Query (server state)
    22→- Heavy libs (Three.js, Chart.js, >50KB): `dynamic(() => import(...), { ssr: false })` with non-null loading fallback
    23→
    24→## B. Next.js 16 Critical Rules (§1.3 — these BREAK code if ignored)
    25→1. `params` / `searchParams` are Promises → `const { slug } = await params`
    26→2. `proxy.ts` replaces `middleware.ts` → NEVER create `src/middleware.ts`
    27→3. `cookies()` / `headers()` are async → `const store = await cookies()`
    28→4. `store.set()` only in Server Actions / Route Handlers, never during render
    29→5. `global-error.tsx` ONLY at `src/app/global-error.tsx` (root), must render own `<html>/<body>`
    30→6. Route groups `(name)` don't affect URL → only ONE page.tsx per URL path (no conflicts)
    31→7. `not-found.tsx` is a server component → use `getTranslations()`, NOT `useTranslations()`
    32→
    33→## C. Architecture (§1.2, §1.5–1.8)
    34→- Structure: `src/app/[locale]/` (layout, page, loading, error, not-found), `components/{ui,layout,providers,feature}`, `lib/{db,auth,brand,domain}.ts`, `i18n/`, `messages/{en,ar}.json`, `prisma/`
    35→- `'use client'` ONLY for hooks/events/browser APIs/router hooks. NEVER import `@/lib/db` or `fs` into client components
    36→- Hydration safety: gate `Date.now()`, `Math.random()`, `localStorage`, timezone formatting behind `mounted` flag + `useEffect`
    37→- Sticky footer: `min-h-screen flex flex-col` on body + `flex-1` main + `mt-auto` footer + `pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]`
    38→- Every async route: `loading.tsx` (role="status" + aria-live + sr-only) + `error.tsx` (no stack traces) + segment `not-found.tsx`
    39→
    40→## D. Security (§2 — defense in depth)
    41→1. Two-layer auth: edge check in `proxy.ts` + `requireAuth()` as FIRST statement of every admin server action/route
    42→2. Zod validation on EVERY API input before Prisma
    43→3. No raw SQL with user input; dynamic orderBy/field names validated against allow-lists
    44→4. Webhooks: HMAC (timingSafeEqual — NOTE: `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual` does NOT exist, use node:crypto) + timestamp skew ≤5min + nonce idempotency in Serializable tx
    45→5. Rate limiting on login/contact/checkout/password reset; IP from trusted proxy header (last in XFF chain)
    46→6. Secrets: .env only, never `NEXT_PUBLIC_` prefixed, ≥32 chars, fail closed at startup
    47→7. IDOR: scope queries by `{ id, userId: session.userId }`; admin scopes by brand from session
    48→8. Cookies: httpOnly + secure(prod) + sameSite:'lax' + `__Host-` prefix; maxAge ≤24h
    49→9. Payment: NEVER trust client price — recompute in Serializable tx with 0.001 tolerance
    50→10. Dev-only endpoints → 404 in production
    51→11. No info leaks: no stack traces, no file paths, no schema names, no exact stock counts
    52→12. Security headers centralized in next.config.ts (nosniff, DENY, referrer-policy, permissions-policy, HSTS, CSP)
    53→
    54→## E. Database/Prisma (§3)
    55→- Money: `Decimal` (not Float); if Float forced → `KWD_TOLERANCE = 0.001` everywhere
    56→- Status fields: Prisma enums, not strings; always `createdAt`/`updatedAt`
    57→- Compound `@@index` for every common `where` combo; FKs NOT auto-indexed in SQLite — add explicitly
    58→- Explicit `onDelete`: Cascade (OrderItem→Order), SetNull (Booking→Product, nullable FK), Restrict (protect parents)
    59→- Multi-tenant: `brand` field on every tenant model; every query scoped by it (from session, not URL)
    60→- Stock check + booking create INTERLEAVED in ONE Serializable transaction (prevents overbooking)
    61→- Overlap condition: `A.start < B.endEff AND A.endEff > B.start` with endOfDay extension on BOTH sides (symmetric!), strict `<`
    62→- PENDING bookings TTL 30min (lazy GC) in availability queries — DoS prevention
    63→- Catch P2002 (unique) / P2025 (not found) / P2034 (tx conflict) with friendly messages; P2002 is the real TOCTOU safety net
    64→- Multi-item orders: shared generated `orderId` (`ORD-...`); webhook confirms ALL via `updateMany({ where: { orderId } })`
    65→- Normalize dates to UTC midnight server-side (`YYYY-MM-DD` → `T00:00:00.000Z`)
    66→- SQLite path consistency: ONE db path everywhere; verify single *.db file exists
    67→
    68→## F. TypeScript & Code Quality (§4)
    69→- strict + noUncheckedIndexedAccess + noImplicitOverride + exactOptionalPropertyTypes
    70→- No `any`/`as any` → use `unknown` + narrowing; no `!` non-null assertions → explicit checks
    71→- No silent catches; loading state reset in `finally` (never only in try or catch)
    72→- React: stable keys (not index), cleanup effects, no derived state in useState, no window reads in render
    73→- Dynamic i18n keys: `t.has(key) ? t(key) : fallback`
    74→- Component size: <100 ideal, <300 acceptable, >500 split
    75→- Brand colors ONCE in `src/lib/brand-colors.ts` + CSS vars via `data-brand`; grep hex values to catch stale refs
    76→- Brand routing helpers in ONE file (`resolveBrandFromPath`, `getProductsBasePath`, `getCartHref`) — never hardcode `/products` in shared components
    77→- Structured JSON logs server-side with `event` + `correlationId`; log IDs, never PII/secrets
    78→
    79→## G. i18n (§5)
    80→- EN/AR perfect key parity (parity diff script in CI)
    81→- camelCase keys ONLY (no snake_case duplicates)
    82→- ICU placeholders; Arabic 6 plural forms (zero/one/two/few/many/other)
    83→- `getTranslations()` in server, `useTranslations()` in client only
    84→- RTL: logical properties only (`ms-`, `me-`, `ps-`, `pe-`, `start-`, `end-`); `<html dir="rtl">`; test every page in RTL
    85→
    86→## H. Booking/Cart/Checkout Flow (§6)
    87→Cart(localStorage, brand-scoped keys) → checkout validation (products active, price recomputed from DB) → POST /api/orders (Serializable tx, interleaved check+create, one orderId) → payment view (fetch ALL bookings by orderId) → webhook (HMAC + idempotency + state machine validation) → updateMany all to CONFIRMED → success page.
    88→- Payment state machine: PENDING→[CONFIRMED|CANCELLED|PAYMENT_FAILED], CONFIRMED→[COMPLETED|CANCELLED], CANCELLED/COMPLETED terminal, PAYMENT_FAILED→retry allowed. Validate transitions BEFORE update.
    89→- Cross-brand cart: reject or clear with toast
    90→
    91→## I. Accessibility (§7 — WCAG 2.1 AA)
    92→- Semantic HTML, keyboard reachable, `focus-visible:ring-2`, 44px touch targets
    93→- aria-label on icon-only buttons WITH context ("Delete Product X")
    94→- Forms: labels, aria-invalid, aria-describedby, errors in `role="alert"` + `text-destructive` (red, never brand color)
    95→- Loading: role="status" + aria-live="polite" + sr-only; toasts: role="alert"
    96→- Modals: focus trap, role="dialog", aria-modal, ESC
    97→- Contrast: 4.5:1 normal text, 3:1 large/UI (no `text-paper/30`-style low opacity)
    98→- 3D/WebGL: dark gradient fallback (no white page on reduced-motion); dynamic import loading ≠ null
    99→- Success states: move focus to message, don't auto-close too fast
   100→- Client-computed values (totals): guard with mounted flag (no 0.000 flash)
   101→
   102→## J. Performance & SEO (§8)
   103→- `next/image` everywhere (alt + width/height or fill+sizes); `next/font` (Inter latin + Cairo arabic, preload:false for rare fonts)
   104→- Tree-shaken icon imports; paginated queries (take/skip); select to limit over-fetch; no N+1 (include/select)
   105→- generateStaticParams + dynamicParams on dynamic routes; ISR revalidate where sensible
   106→- Centralized `buildMetadata` with `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` (never hardcode URLs)
   107→- `src/app/sitemap.ts` (all 200-OK URLs only, hreflang alternates, ALL brands) and `src/app/robots.ts` (delete `public/robots.txt` to avoid shadowing)
   108→- JSON-LD (Organization/WebSite) on HOME page ONLY; Product+BreadcrumbList on product pages
   109→
   110→## K. Common Pitfalls Catalog (§9 — symptom → fix quick map)
   111→- Next.js 16: params undefined→await Promise; same-path conflict→one page per URL; global-error at root; no middleware.ts
   112→- Security: missing requireAuth; webhook idempotency; PENDING TTL; server-side price recompute; generic stock messages; NODE_ENV gate; P2002 catch; cookie flags
   113→- DB: compound indexes; interleaved Serializable tx; symmetric +24h overlap; onDelete SetNull; single db file path; Decimal for money; shared orderId for multi-item
   114→- Quality: no `!`, finally resets, no silent catch, no `as any`, BRAND_COLORS const, brand path helpers, no dead code, t.has() guard
   115→- i18n: parity, one case convention, getTranslations in server, logical properties, 6 Arabic plurals
   116→- a11y: 3D fallback, contextual aria-labels, mounted guard, focus management, destructive color for errors
   117→- SEO: JSON-LD home only, env URLs, no redirects in sitemap, robots.ts not static file, all brands in sitemap, hreflang
   118→- Perf: dynamic ssr:false for heavy libs, no N+1, pagination, select, next/font, next/image
   119→- Dev env: background dev server with keepalive, bun install after wipes, db:push+seed after db loss, pkill next dev on port conflict, rm -rf .next on stale cache
   120→
   121→## L. Loops Audit Methodology (§10)
   122→- Group 1 (read-only analysis): 4 parallel agents — Security / Code Quality / Architecture / Data+i18n — cite file:line, severity (CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/INFO), concrete fix
   123→- Group 2 (fixers): parallel, non-overlapping file ownership, minimal surgical changes, lint+tsc after, worklog entry
   124→- Round 2: re-verify (FIXED/PARTIALLY/NOT FIXED + regression hunt) — loop until all PASS
   125→- Group 3: 6 fresh reviewers (Security, Business Logic, Architecture, TypeScript, Data/i18n, UI/UX a11y) — fresh eyes, loop until all 6 PASS
   126→- Severity: CRITICAL blocks release, HIGH fix before release, MEDIUM next sprint, LOW backlog, INFO note
   127→
   128→## M. Definition of Done (§11 + Appendix C) — "It compiles" is NEVER enough
   129→Checklist: lint 0/0 · tsc 0 errors · i18n parity 0 diff · requireAuth everywhere · Zod on inputs · webhook 3-layer defense · rate limits · cookie flags · no NEXT_PUBLIC secrets · NODE_ENV gates · indexes · onDelete · Serializable interleaved tx · PENDING TTL · symmetric overlap · shared orderId · P2002/P2025/P2034 handled · await params · proxy.ts not middleware · global-error at root · one page per URL · generateStaticParams · metadata per page · minimal use client · dynamic heavy libs · no hydration mismatch · loading/error files · sticky footer · no any/! /silent catch · finally resets · no dead code · BRAND_COLORS · path helpers · <500 line files · parity · 6 Arabic plurals · getTranslations server-side · logical props · a11y full checklist · sitemap/robots correctness · JSON-LD home only · next/image+font · pagination+select · BROWSER-VERIFIED interactivity (render, click, submit, real data, responsive, sticky footer, RTL, no console errors).
   130→
   131→## N. Key Commands (Appendix A)
   132→- Background dev server: `nohup setsid bash -c 'while true; do bun run dev >> dev.log 2>&1; sleep 3; done' < /dev/null > /dev/null 2>&1 & disown`
   133→- `bun run lint` · `bunx tsc --noEmit` · `bun run db:push` (dev only) · `bun run db:seed` · `bunx prisma migrate deploy` (prod)
   134→- i18n parity node one-liner (see guide) · find *.db (exactly one expected)
   135→
   136→## O. Testing Priority (Appendix D — despite "no test code" default, pure logic verification matters)
   137→Tier 1 pure functions first: overlaps/endOfDay boundary matrix, computeDays+tolerance, canTransition edges, brand path helpers. Tier 2 API integration (overbooking, price mismatch, webhook staleness/dup-nonce). Tier 3 one e2e smoke per critical path in BOTH locales.

   235→Task ID: PHASE-2
   236→Agent: Main (Z.ai Code)
   237→Task: Execute Phase 2 "Sensory Polish Layer" (elyra-fullstack-prompt-02.md): magnetic cursor + film grain + Audio UX + content enrichment + React Compiler + hreflang sitemap + Lighthouse tuning.
   238→
   239→Work Log:
   240→- **Magnetic Cursor** (`src/components/sensory/magnetic-cursor.tsx`): two layers (6px dot exact + 32px ring lerp 0.2) driven by ONE rAF loop writing `translate3d` via refs (zero React re-renders). Magnet snap within 80px of `[data-cursor="magnet"]` centers (distance-based → RTL/LTR neutral). Guards: `pointer:fine` + NOT reduced-motion + client-only. Native cursor hidden only under those conditions (double CSS media guard). Layers: `pointer-events:none`, `aria-hidden`, z-200 ABOVE Sheet/Dialog (native hidden → must stay visible over modals), `mix-blend-difference` for light/dark visibility. Magnet rects cached, refreshed on scroll/resize (rAF-throttled) + debounced MutationObserver for client navigations. Press feedback (scale 0.82) on pointerdown.
   241→- **Film Grain** (`film-grain.tsx` + `.elyra-grain` in globals.css): static SVG feTurbulence data-URI, fixed inset-0, opacity 3.5% (WCAG-safe), pointer-events:none, aria-hidden, hidden @media print, single instance in layout, z-90. CSP img-src already allows data:.

   341→  - P1-4 bug 1: animated value was the wrong number. The original RingGauge animated displayValue from 0 to 'fraction' (a 0-1 number), then formatted that as money via formatValue(n). So a $15000 budget with fraction=0.75 showed as '$1'. Fix: split 'fraction' (ring fill, 0-1) from 'value' (count-up display, the actual money/weeks number). Calculator now passes BOTH: fraction=Math.min(1, result.max/20000) AND value=result.max.
   342→  - P1-4 bug 2: animation never ran in React Strict Mode. The original useEffect set fromRef.current = to BEFORE the rAF actually fired. Strict Mode runs effect setup → cleanup → effect setup again. First setup updated fromRef but its cleanup cancelled the rAF before it could tick. Second setup saw from === to → early-returned → no animation at all → rings stayed at 0 forever. Fix: only commit fromRef.current = to INSIDE the tick callback when the animation completes. Added a 'cancelled' flag so the cleanup can signal the in-flight tick to bail without scheduling another rAF.

  1264→Stage Summary:
  1265→FINDINGS:
  1266→
  1267→[P1] .git/config (environment; repo remote) — Live GitHub access token embedded in the origin remote URL
  1268→  Evidence: `git remote -v` → `https://x-access-token:<redacted>@github.com/ashamsaldin303-ctrl/nextjs-fullstack-project.git` (credential detected in the stored remote URL; value never printed).
  1269→  Impact: Any process, backup, CI log, or pasted `git remote -v` output that reads .git/config yields a usable GitHub credential for that repo (push/fetch scope unverified). It is a plaintext secret at rest on disk.
  1270→  Recommended fix: `git remote set-url origin https://github.com/ashamsaldin303-ctrl/nextjs-fullstack-project.git` and authenticate via credential helper or SSH deploy key instead; rotate the token if it was ever copied anywhere.
  1271→
  1272→[P2] prisma/schema.prisma:15-17 + scripts/clean-leads.ts:20 + README Deployment — Lead PII (ipAddress, userAgent, email, whatsapp, message) retained indefinitely; promised retention policy does not exist
  1273→  Evidence: schema comment says "Define a retention policy before real production use (e.g., purge Lead rows older than 90 days) — see README 'Deployment'"; README has NO retention/purge text (rg 'retention|purge|90 days' → 0 hits) and clean-leads.ts only does `deleteMany({})` as a pre-launch full wipe; nothing is scheduled.
  1274→  Impact: Privacy/compliance exposure (IP + UA + full contact text are personal data under GDPR-like regimes); SQLite grows forever; a leaked DB file exposes the complete contact history.
  1275→  Recommended fix: Add an age-based purge job (cron/GitHub Action running `prisma.lead.deleteMany({ where: { createdAt: { lt: <cutoff> } } })`, or null out ipAddress/userAgent after 30–90 days keeping the business fields), and document the policy in README Deployment where the schema comment points.
  1276→
  1277→[P2] Root reverse-proxy file :6-18 — Shipped proxy config contains an intentional open-proxy/SSRF primitive (?XTransformPort)
  1278→  Evidence: `reverse_proxy localhost:{query.XTransformPort}` proxies any request carrying `?XTransformPort=<port>` to an arbitrary localhost port; header block explicitly warns "SANDBOX-ONLY … NEVER deploy this publicly".
  1279→  Impact: An operator copy-pasting the repo's only provided proxy config into production exposes an SSRF relay into every localhost service on the host (databases, n8n, admin panels). The app's own TRUST_PROXY/XFF-overwrite guidance points users at this exact file.
  1280→  Recommended fix: Move the sandbox file to `sandbox/` (or gate the transform behind a build flag) and add a production-safe `deploy/` variant (TLS block, no XTransformPort, XFF overwrite only); mention the split in README Deployment.
  1281→
  1282→[P2] route.ts:149-155 + rate-limit.ts:23-25 + Dockerfile:52-61 + README docker-run — Documented production deployment runs with TRUST_PROXY unset → single GLOBAL rate-limit bucket → trivial total lockout of the only conversion endpoint
  1283→  Evidence: `if (process.env.TRUST_PROXY !== 'true') return 'anonymous'` — with TRUST_PROXY unset/false every caller shares one bucket (30 lenient / 5 strict per MINUTE, globally). The Dockerfile ENV block does not set TRUST_PROXY and the README `docker run` example passes only DATABASE_URL/N8N_* vars; TRUST_PROXY appears in no deployment doc except a comment in the root proxy file (rg confirms: that comment + .env.example:31=false).
  1284→  Impact: ~30 junk POSTs/min from one curl loop keeps the shared lenient bucket permanently exhausted → every legitimate visitor gets 429 on BOTH forms site-wide, continuously. Availability of the lead pipeline hinges on an env var the canonical deploy recipe never sets.
  1285→  Recommended fix: Set `TRUST_PROXY=true` in the Dockerfile/README docker-run example (deployment is by definition behind the overwriting proxy), or reconsider the fail-closed default for the standalone Docker target; at minimum add TRUST_PROXY to the README run command with one explanatory line.
  1286→
  1287→[P2] next.config.ts:26-38 (and 41-55) — Production CSP keeps 'unsafe-inline' in script-src; no COOP/CORP headers
  1288→  Evidence: `"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'"` with the documented rationale (static prerender embeds inline bootstrap; nonce CSP incompatible). securityHeaders list has no Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy / Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy. (HSTS is emitted while the sandbox serves plain HTTP — harmless, browsers ignore it there; correct once TLS terminates at the proxy.)
  1289→  Impact: Any future HTML-injection bug escalates to full XSS with no CSP backstop (unsafe-inline voids script-src protection); missing COOP leaves the site without window.opener isolation. Current exploitability is LOW (React auto-escaping everywhere, no user-content HTML sink), so this is a hardening gap, not an active hole.
  1290→  Recommended fix: Add `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin` and `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin` now; evaluate hash-based CSP for the stable inline bootstrap chunks, or make the shell dynamic to enable nonce CSP. Keep the documented tradeoff note updated with whatever is chosen.
  1291→
  1292→[P3] route.ts:287-305 — Body-size gate depends on content-length/transfer-encoding presence; length-less h2 forwarding would bypass it
  1293→  Evidence: 413 fires only when `content-length > 64 KB` or `transfer-encoding` is present; a request with NEITHER header (possible over HTTP/2 DATA frames) skips both gates and `req.json()` buffers the full body before Zod runs.
  1294→  Impact: Unreachable in the shipped stack (Node standalone = HTTP/1.1; the fronting proxy speaks h1 to upstream with CL/chunked), but a future h2c/h2-direct exposure turns this into a memory-DoS primitive.
  1295→  Recommended fix: Document "never expose the app over h2 directly / proxy must forward length framing", or wrap the parse with a size-checked stream read (e.g. read the body via a capped reader before JSON.parse).
  1296→
  1297→[P3] git history (commits 6d367b6 → b659e9b) — .env was committed then deleted; history still contains it
  1298→  Evidence: `git log --all --name-status -- .env` → `A .env` in 6d367b6, `D .env` in b659e9b. Key names verified WITH VALUES REDACTED: it contained exactly one key, DATABASE_URL (local SQLite path — not a credential).
  1299→  Impact: Minimal today (no secret value), but the pattern is a footgun and the file remains retrievable from history if the repo goes public.
  1300→  Recommended fix: No action needed for a file path; if the repo will be public, rewrite history (git filter-repo/BFG) for hygiene and keep `.env*` ignored (already .gitignore:35).
  1301→
  1302→[P3] n8n-webhook.ts:94-99 / :179 — deliver() takes a `secret` parameter it never uses
  1303→  Evidence: `async function deliver(url, secret, body, headers)` — the body of deliver() never reads `secret`; called as `deliver(config.url, config.secret, body, headers)`.
  1304→  Impact: No vulnerability (server-side only), but it needlessly propagates the HMAC secret through a call stack that doesn't need it — the kind of parameter that later gets logged by mistake.
  1305→  Recommended fix: Drop the `secret` parameter from deliver() and its call site.
  1306→
  1307→[P3] rate-limit.ts:36-54 — Transient Map growth possible between throttled sweeps
  1308→  Evidence: `sweep()` executes at most once per 60 s (`if (now - lastSweep < SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS) return`); within that interval every NEW distinct key inserts without any size check; the 5 000-key cap only applies when the sweep body actually runs.
  1309→  Impact: Requires TRUST_PROXY=true AND direct reachability past the overwriting proxy (i.e., misdeployment) — an attacker minting fresh XFF keys can grow the Map unbounded for up to 60 s before the cap fires. Steady state is capped; realistic risk is low.
  1310→  Recommended fix: Cheap hardening — when inserting a key that didn't previously exist, also check `hits.size > 10_000` and run the eviction inline (or skip inserting the new key entirely).
  1311→
  1312→[P3] robots.ts:9 — Disallow list advertises a nonexistent /admin path

  1317→[P3] src/components/seo/home-json-ld.tsx:14-55 — JSON-LD embedded via dangerouslySetInnerHTML without `</script>` escaping
  1318→  Evidence: `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify({...}) }}` — JSON.stringify does NOT escape `</script>`, so any value containing it would break out of the script element. All current inputs are build-time constants (SITE_URL env, SITE_CONTACT/SITE_SOCIAL literals).
  1319→  Impact: Safe as shipped (no user-controlled data reaches it), but the pattern breaks silently the day a dynamic value is added.
  1320→  Recommended fix: Wrap with `.replace(/</g, '\\u003c')` on the stringified output (standard JSON-LD hardening).

  1436→[P1] capability-scene.tsx:462 — Mouse parallax can never activate (R3F hijacks e.currentTarget)
  1437→  Evidence: `const rect = (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).getBoundingClientRect?.()` … `if (rect) { mouse.current.tx = … }` — in @react-three/fiber v9 the raycast event overrides BOTH target and currentTarget with `{ hasPointerCapture, setPointerCapture, releasePointerCapture }` (verified in node_modules/@react-three/fiber/dist/events-156d8d12.esm.js:765-773); that object has NO getBoundingClientRect, so `rect` is always undefined and the only writes to mouse.current.tx/ty (lines 464-465) never execute. The useFrame parallax (lines 416-421) then lerps toward (0,0) forever.
  1438→  Impact: The documented "mouse parallax lerp (now on a parent group so blob+satellites+halo drift together)" is a dead feature — the scene never responds to hover position (only auto-spin + drag still work).
  1439→  Recommended fix: Drop manual rect math entirely — read the R3F-maintained NDC pointer inside useFrame: `const { gl, pointer } = state; parallax.current.position.x += (pointer.x * viewport.width / 4 - parallax.current.position.x) * 0.04` (state.pointer is updated by R3F's event pass; no listener needed). Delete lines 462-466 and the mouse ref.

  1454→  Recommended fix: Either add the interaction (drag delta on the group with clamped rotation, mirroring capability-scene's throttled pattern) or fix the affordance: drop data-cursor="rotate" + change the chip label + remove `cursor: 'grab'`, and reword the header comment.
  1455→
  1456→[P2] hero-canvas.tsx:39 + 583-598 — No mobile tier: 4500 curl-noise particles + 15-vnoise silk fragment ship to phones
  1457→  Evidence: `const PARTICLE_COUNT = 4500` (fixed); SILK_FRAGMENT runs 3× fbm×5 octaves = 15 value-noise samples (~60 sin) per pixel; hero.tsx gates only on `!reduced && load3D` — no viewport/mobile matchMedia (unlike hero-console, which replaces WebGL with SVG below 768px); the only mobile concession is `dpr={[1,2]}`.
  1458→  Impact: Low-end phones render ~1.3M px × heavy fragment math + 4500-particle vertex curl (6 snoise/vertex) at up to 60fps — jank/battery risk; prior rounds never reduced the count.
  1459→  Recommended fix: Add a coarse-pointer or <768px tier: PARTICLE_COUNT 4500→1800 and dpr [1,1.5] (or a uniform-driven fbm octave drop to 4 is harder — count+dpr is enough); alternatively reuse hero-console's `matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)')` to skip the silk plane on phones (particles alone read fine over the CSS fallback).

  1748→[P3] src/components/home/console-scene.tsx:463-574 (+ hero-console.tsx:281-309) — dragActiveRef can strand true if ConsoleScene unmounts mid-drag
  1749→  Evidence: ConsoleScene calls `onDragStateChange?.(true)` in onPointerDown and `?.(false)` only in endDrag (pointerup/pointercancel). hero-console renders `{showSvg ? <ConsoleSvgDiagram/> : <><ConsoleScene …/>…</>}` — when showSvg flips false→true the WebGL branch (and its pointer handlers) unmounts with no cleanup reporting false. Flip sources: isMobile MQL crossing 768px (window resize mid-drag), reduced-motion OS toggle mid-drag (webglSupported cannot flip true→false after resolving).
  1750→  Impact: with a pending payoff navigation, the navigate chain postpones forever (dragActiveRef stays true) AND `if (navTimer.current) return` blocks every later intent — the hero's conversion path is dead until page reload. Freak-probability trigger, high-impact result.
  1751→  Recommended fix: one line in ConsoleScene — `useEffect(() => () => onDragStateChange?.(false), [onDragStateChange])` (reportDragState is stable, so the cleanup fires exactly on unmount; idempotent when no drag was active).
  1752→[P3] src/components/home/hero-canvas.tsx:424 — Particles uPixelRatio is not tier-aware (point-size/render-scale mismatch introduced by the mobile tier)
  1753→  Evidence: `uPixelRatio: { value: Math.min(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.devicePixelRatio : 1, 2) }` (captured once per mount, memo deps []), while the mobile tier clamps the RENDERER to `dpr={[1,1.5]}`. On a ≥2×-DPR phone: renderer 1.5 vs uniform 2 → gl_PointSize scales 2/1.5 ≈ 1.33× larger than intended relative to render scale (pre-tier code was consistent at dpr 2 / uniform 2).
  1754→  Impact: cosmetic only — mobile-tier particle sprites render ~33% oversize relative to the scene on retina phones; no correctness/perf effect (perf goal still achieved via count 1800 + dpr 1.5).
  1755→  Recommended fix: clamp the uniform with the tier — e.g. pass `dprMax` as a prop to Particles (`uPixelRatio: { value: Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, dprMax) }`); the existing key remount on tier flip already re-runs the memo.
